    this.SkipText()

  L_4721:

}
r2.StartHideMenu = function () {
    this.SubtitleText._visible = false
    this.bFadedIn = false
    this.SpeakerName.SetText(" ")
    this.ExitButton._visible = false
    this._parent.gotoAndPlay("startFadeOut")
    gfx.io.GameDelegate.call("CloseMenu", [])
}
